(\theta_{i}-\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4},\theta_{i}+\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4})\subset(\theta_{i}-\varphi_{i},\theta_{i}+\varphi_{i})
, and
\Lambda_{i}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta_{i},\varphi_{i})}\subset{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta_{i},\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4})}
,
we get

\displaystyle\rho\;\;\circ_{(\theta_{i},\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4})\Lambda_{i}\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{i}}\}}\;\;\sigma=\rho\;\;\circ_{(\theta_{i},{\varphi_{i}})\Lambda_{i}\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{i}}\}}\;\;\sigma

(3.6)

by
2.
above.
Choose some
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)

such that
(\theta_{i}-\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4},\theta_{i}+\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4})\subset(\theta-\varphi,\theta+\varphi)
,

i=1,2
.(These exist because
\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4}<\frac{\pi}{4}
.)
Fix some
\Lambda_{0}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}\subset{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta_{i},\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4})}

and
\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\mid\eta\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}\}
.
Then from
2.
, we have

\displaystyle\rho\;\;\circ_{(\theta_{i},\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4})\Lambda_{0}\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\}}\;\;\sigma=\rho\;\;\circ_{(\theta,{\varphi})\Lambda_{0}\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\}}\;\;\sigma.

(3.7)

Replacing

\theta,\varphi,\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{2}}

with

\theta_{i},\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4},\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{i},\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}},\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{i}}
,
we apply
1.
and obtain

\displaystyle\rho\;\;\circ_{(\theta_{i},\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4})\Lambda_{0}\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\}}\;\;\sigma\simeq_{u.e.}\rho\;\;\circ_{(\theta_{i},\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4})\Lambda_{i}\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{i}}\}}\;\;\sigma.

(3.8)

Combining (
3.6
), (
3.7
), (
3.8
), we complete the proof of the Lemma.

\square

Lemma 3.3
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
For any
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,

\Lambda_{0}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

\rho,\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and

\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\mid\eta\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}\}
,

\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma
defined in Definition
3.1

belongs to
{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
.
In particular, we have

\displaystyle\rho\;\;\circ_{(\bar{\theta},\bar{\varphi}),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\}}\;\;\sigma|_{{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda_{0}^{c}}}=\pi_{0}|_{{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda_{0}^{c}}}.

(3.9)

Let
\Lambda
be a cone in
{\mathbb{R}}^{2}
.
For this cone, there exist
\bar{\theta}\in{\mathbb{R}}
,

\bar{\varphi}\in(0,\pi)
, such that
\Lambda\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\bar{\theta},\bar{\varphi})}
.
Fix some

\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\eta,\Lambda}\mid\eta\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}\}
.
By the definition, we have

\displaystyle\rho\;\;\circ_{(\bar{\theta},\bar{\varphi}),\Lambda,\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda}\}}\;\;\sigma|_{{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda^{c}}}=T_{\rho}^{(\bar{\theta},\bar{\varphi})\Lambda\bar{V}_{\rho\Lambda}}\circ T_{\sigma}^{(\bar{\theta},\bar{\varphi})\Lambda\bar{V}_{\sigma\Lambda}}\circ\pi_{0}|_{{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda^{c}}}=\pi_{0}|_{{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda^{c}}}

(3.10)

using Lemma
2.14
(d).
From Lemma
3.2
, we have

\displaystyle\rho\;\;\circ_{(\bar{\theta},\bar{\varphi}),\Lambda,\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda}\}}\;\;\sigma\simeq_{u.e.}\rho\;\;\circ_{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\}}\;\;\sigma.

(3.11)

Hence we have

\displaystyle\rho\;\;\circ_{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\}}\;\;\sigma|_{{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda^{c}}}\simeq_{u.e.}\rho\;\;\circ_{(\bar{\theta},\bar{\varphi}),\Lambda,\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda}\}}\;\;\sigma|_{{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda^{c}}}=\pi_{0}|_{{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda^{c}}}.

(3.12)

As this holds for any cone
\Lambda
, we obtain
\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
.

\square

The product preserves the unitary equivalence.

Lemma 3.4
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,

\Lambda_{0}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

\rho_{1},\rho_{2},\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and

\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\mid\eta\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}\}
.
Suppose that
\rho_{1}\simeq_{u.e.}\rho_{2}
and
\sigma_{1}\simeq_{u.e.}\sigma_{2}
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\rho_{1}\;\;\circ_{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\}}\;\;\sigma_{1}\simeq_{u.e.}\rho_{2}\;\;\circ_{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\}}\;\;\sigma_{2}.

(3.13)

Let
W_{\rho},W_{\sigma}
be unitaries such that

\rho_{2}=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits(W_{\rho})\circ\rho_{1}

and
\sigma_{2}=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits(W_{\sigma})\circ\sigma_{1}
.
From the proof of Lemma
2.15
(i) (
2.47
),
we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{0}}}=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{0}}W_{\rho}^{*}\bar{V}_{\rho_{2},\Lambda_{0}}^{*}\right)\circ T_{\rho_{2}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho_{2},\Lambda_{0}}},\\
&T_{\sigma_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma_{1},\Lambda_{0}}}=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\sigma_{1},\Lambda_{0}}W_{\sigma}^{*}\bar{V}_{\sigma_{2},\Lambda_{0}}^{*}\right)\circ T_{\sigma_{2}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma_{2},\Lambda_{0}}}.\end{split}

(3.14)

Because
W_{\sigma}^{*}
is an intertwiner from
\sigma_{2}
to
\sigma_{1}
,
from Lemma
2.2
,

\bar{V}_{\sigma_{1},\Lambda_{0}}W_{\sigma}^{*}\bar{V}_{\sigma_{2},\Lambda_{0}}^{*}

belongs to
\pi_{0}({\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda_{0}^{c}})^{\prime}=\mathfrak{A}(\Lambda_{0})\subset{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\rho_{1}\;\;\circ_{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\}}\;\;\sigma_{1}=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{0}}W_{\rho}^{*}\bar{V}_{\rho_{2},\Lambda_{0}}^{*}T_{\rho_{2}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho_{2},\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\bar{V}_{\sigma_{1},\Lambda_{0}}W_{\sigma}^{*}\bar{V}_{\sigma_{2},\Lambda_{0}}^{*}\right)\right)\circ\rho_{2}\;\;\circ_{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\}}\;\;\sigma_{2}.

(3.15)

This proves the Lemma.

\square

Lemma 3.5
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
.
Let
\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

\rho,\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
,
and
V_{\rho\Lambda_{i}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho\Lambda_{i}}
,
V_{\sigma\Lambda_{i}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{i}}
,

i=1,2
.
Then for any
\Lambda\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}}\circ T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}}

is
\sigma
w-continuous on
\mathfrak{A}(\Lambda)
.

Let
\Lambda\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
and
0<\varepsilon<\frac{2\pi-|\arg\Lambda|}{4}
.
We have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}}\left(\mathfrak{A}(\Lambda)\right)\subset\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}}\left(U_{\Lambda,\varepsilon}\right)\right)\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}}\left(\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\left(\Lambda-R_{|\Lambda|,\varepsilon}\bm{e}_{\Lambda}\right)_{\varepsilon}}\right)^{\prime\prime}\right)\right)\\
&\subset\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}}\left(U_{\Lambda,\varepsilon}\right)\right)\left(\mathfrak{A}\left(C_{\left(\Lambda-R_{|\Lambda|,\varepsilon}\bm{e}_{\Lambda}\right)_{\varepsilon}}\right)\right)\end{split}

(3.16)

for some cone
C_{\left(\Lambda-R_{|\Lambda|,\varepsilon}\bm{e}_{\Lambda}\right)_{\varepsilon}}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
.
(Lemma
2.14
(e)).
By the
\sigma
w-continuity of
T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}}
on

\mathfrak{A}\left(C_{\left(\Lambda-R_{|\Lambda|,\varepsilon}\bm{e}_{\Lambda}\right)_{\varepsilon}}\right)

and the
\sigma
w-continuity of

T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}}

on
\mathfrak{A}(\Lambda)
(Lemma
2.17
)

T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}}\circ T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}}
is

\sigma
w-continuous on
\mathfrak{A}(\Lambda)
.

\square

Lemma 3.6
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,

\Lambda_{0}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

\rho,\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and

\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\mid\eta\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}\}
.
Set

\gamma:=\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}.

Then we have

\displaystyle T_{\gamma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\gamma,\Lambda_{0}}}=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\gamma\Lambda_{0}}\right)\circ T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\circ T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}},

(3.17)

1\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\gamma,\Lambda_{0}}
,
and
\bar{V}_{\gamma\Lambda_{0}}\in\mathfrak{A}(\Lambda_{0})\subset{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
.

The map
X:=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\gamma\Lambda_{0}}\right)\circ T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\circ T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}:{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}\to{\mathcal{B}}({\mathcal{H}})
is a

*
-homomorphism.
By Lemma
3.5
,

X
is

\sigma
w-continuous on
\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda}\right)^{\prime\prime}
for any
\Lambda\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
.
Furthermore, we have
X\circ\pi_{0}=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\gamma\Lambda_{0}}\right)\circ\gamma
.
Hence by the uniqueness in Lemma
2.14
(a), we obtain
X=T_{\gamma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\gamma,\Lambda_{0}}}
.
As in (
3.10
), we have
\gamma|_{{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda_{0}^{c}}}=\pi_{0}|_{{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda_{0}^{c}}}
.
This means
1\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\gamma,\Lambda_{0}}
.
Therefore, we have

\bar{V}_{\gamma\Lambda_{0}}=\bar{V}_{\gamma\Lambda_{0}}\cdot 1^{*}\in\mathfrak{A}(\Lambda_{0}),

by Lemma
2.2
.

\square

Finally we obtain the associativity in the following sense.

Lemma 3.7
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,

\Lambda_{0}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

\rho,\sigma,\gamma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and

\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\mid\eta\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}\}
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\left(\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma\right)\circ\;\;_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\gamma\simeq_{u.e.}\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\left(\sigma\circ\;\;_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\gamma\right).

(3.18)

Set
\alpha:=\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma

and
\beta:=\sigma\circ\;\;_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\gamma
.
Applying Lemma
3.6
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left(\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma\right)\circ\;\;_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\gamma=T_{\alpha}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\alpha,\Lambda_{0}}}\circ T_{\gamma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\gamma,\Lambda_{0}}}\circ\pi_{0}\\
&=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\alpha\Lambda_{0}}\right)T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\gamma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\gamma,\Lambda_{0}}}\pi_{0}=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\alpha\Lambda_{0}}\right)T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\beta\Lambda_{0}}^{*}\right)T_{\beta}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\beta,\Lambda_{0}}}\pi_{0}\\
&=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\alpha\Lambda_{0}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\bar{V}_{\beta\Lambda_{0}}^{*}\right)\right)T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\beta}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\beta,\Lambda_{0}}}\pi_{0}\\
&=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(\bar{V}_{\alpha\Lambda_{0}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\bar{V}_{\beta\Lambda_{0}}^{*}\right)\right)\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\left(\sigma\circ\;\;_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\gamma\right).\end{split}

(3.19)

In the fourth equality, we used
\bar{V}_{\beta\Lambda_{0}}\in{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
, Lemma
3.6
.

\square

4
The intertwiners

For endomorphisms
T_{1},T_{2}
of
{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
, we denote by

(T_{1},T_{2})
the set of all intertwiners from
T_{1}
to
T_{2}
, i.e.,
the set of all bounded operators
R
on
{\mathcal{H}}
such that
RT_{1}(x)=T_{2}(x)R
,
for all
x\in{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
.

Lemma 4.1
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,

\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

\rho\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and

\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{i}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho,\Lambda_{i}}
,
i=1,2
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{2}}\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}},T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{2}}}\right).

(4.1)

By Lemma
2.14
(ii), we have

\displaystyle\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{2}}\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}^{*}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}}(\pi_{0}(A))=T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{2}}}(\pi_{0}(A))\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{2}}\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}^{*},\quad A\in{\mathcal{A}}_{{\mathbb{Z}}^{2}}.

(4.2)

From the
\sigma
w-continuity of
T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{i}}}
,
i=1,2
on
\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda}\right)^{\prime\prime}
,

\Lambda\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
(Lemma
2.14
(i)), this equality extends to any
\pi_{0}({\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda})^{\prime\prime}

and hence to
{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
by Lemma
2.4
.

\square

Lemma 4.2
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,

\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

\rho,\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and

\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}
,

\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho,\Lambda_{2}}
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}},T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}}\right)\subset\pi_{0}({\mathcal{A}}_{(\Lambda_{1}\cup\Lambda_{2})^{c}})^{\prime}\subset{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}.

(4.3)

For
R\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}},T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}}\right)
we have

\displaystyle R\pi_{0}(A)=RT_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}}(\pi_{0}(A))=T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}}(\pi_{0}(A))R=\pi_{0}(A)R,\quad A\in{\mathcal{A}}_{\left(\Lambda_{1}\cup\Lambda_{2}\right)^{c}}

(4.4)

because of Lemma
2.14
(d).

\square

Lemma 4.3
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,

\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{1}^{\prime},\Lambda_{2},\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

\rho,\rho^{\prime},\sigma,\sigma^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and

V_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}
,
V_{\rho^{\prime},\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho^{\prime},\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}}
,

V_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}
,

V_{\sigma^{\prime},\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\sigma^{\prime},\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}}
.
Then for any
R_{1}\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}},T_{\rho^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}^{\prime},\bar{V}_{\rho^{\prime},\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}}}\right)
and

R_{2}\in\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}},T_{\sigma^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}^{\prime},\bar{V}_{\sigma^{\prime},\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}}}\right)
, we
have

\displaystyle R_{1}\otimes R_{2}:=R_{1}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}}(R_{2})\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}}\circ T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}}},T_{\rho^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}^{\prime},\bar{V}_{\rho^{\prime},\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}}}\circ T_{\sigma^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}^{\prime},\bar{V}_{\sigma^{\prime},\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}}}\right),

(4.5)

and

\displaystyle\left(R_{1}\otimes R_{2}\right)^{*}=R_{1}^{*}\otimes R_{2}^{*}.

(4.6)

Note from Lemma
4.2
,

T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}}(R_{2})
is well-defined.

This follows directly from the definition.

\square

Definition 4.4
.

In the above setting, we define
R_{1}\otimes R_{2}
by (
4.5
).

Lemma 4.5
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,

\Lambda_{i},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime\prime}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

\rho_{i},\rho_{i}^{\prime},\rho_{i}^{\prime\prime}\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and

V_{\rho_{i},\Lambda_{i}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{i},\Lambda_{i}}
,

V_{\rho_{i}^{\prime},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{i}^{\prime},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}}

V_{\rho_{i}^{\prime\prime},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime\prime}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{i}^{\prime\prime},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime\prime}}
,
i=1,2
.
Then for any
R_{i}\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{i}}},T_{\rho^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i}^{\prime},\bar{V}_{\rho^{\prime},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}}}\right)
and

R_{i}^{\prime}\in\left(T_{\rho^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i},\bar{V}_{\rho^{\prime},\Lambda_{i}}},T_{\rho^{\prime\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i}^{\prime},\bar{V}_{\rho^{\prime\prime},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}}}\right)

i=1,2
,
we have

\displaystyle R_{1}^{\prime}R_{1}\otimes R_{2}^{\prime}R_{2}=(R_{1}^{\prime}\otimes R_{2}^{\prime})(R_{1}\otimes R_{2}).

(4.7)

This follows directly from the definition.

\square

Lemma 4.6
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,

\Lambda_{i},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,
\rho_{i},\rho_{i}^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and

\bar{V}_{\rho_{i},\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{i},\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}
,

\bar{V}_{\rho_{i}^{\prime},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}+{t_{i}^{\prime}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{i}^{\prime},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}+{t_{i}^{\prime}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}
,

t_{i},t_{i}^{\prime}\geq 0
,
i=1,2
.
Suppose that
\{\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}\}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\{\Lambda_{2},\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}\}
.
Suppse

S_{i}^{t_{i},t_{i}^{\prime}}\in\left(T_{\rho_{i}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{i},\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}},T_{\rho^{\prime}_{i}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}+t_{i}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}},\bar{V}_{\rho^{\prime}_{i},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}+t_{i}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}}}}\right),\quad i=1,2

with
\left\|S_{i}^{t_{i}t_{i}^{\prime}}\right\|\leq 1

is given
for each
t_{i},t_{i}^{\prime}\geq 0
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\lim_{t_{1},t_{2}\to\infty}\left\|S_{1}^{t_{1},t_{1}^{\prime}}\otimes S_{2}^{t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}}-S_{2}^{t_{2}t_{2}^{\prime}}\otimes S_{1}^{t_{1}t_{1}^{\prime}}\right\|=0.

(4.8)

By the definition (Definition
2.6
), there are
\tilde{\Lambda}_{1},\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
and
\varepsilon>0

such that
\Lambda_{i},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}\subset\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}
,
i=1,2
,
and

\displaystyle\begin{split}\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}-R_{\left|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\right|,\varepsilon}\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}}\right)_{\varepsilon}\subset\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)^{c},\quad\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}-R_{\left|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right|,\varepsilon}\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}\right)_{\varepsilon}\subset\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\right)^{c},\\
\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)_{\varepsilon},\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\right)_{\varepsilon}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)},\quad\arg\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)_{\varepsilon}\cap\arg\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\right)_{\varepsilon}=\emptyset.\end{split}

(4.9)

Choose
\delta>0
small enough so that
(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1})_{\varepsilon+\delta},(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2})_{\varepsilon+\delta}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}

and
\arg\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)_{\varepsilon+\delta}\cap\arg\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\right)_{\varepsilon+\delta}=\emptyset.

For any
t\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}+t_{i}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}}\subset\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}},\quad i=1,2,\\
&\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+\frac{t}{2}\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}}\right)_{\varepsilon+\delta}\subset\left(\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{c},\quad\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}+\frac{t}{2}\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}\right)_{\varepsilon+\delta}\subset\left(\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{c}\end{split}

(4.10)

for
t_{1},t_{2},t_{1}^{\prime},t_{2}^{\prime}
large enough by Lemma
A.2
.
For such
t_{1},t_{2},t_{1}^{\prime},t_{2}^{\prime}
given for
t\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+\left(t-R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}|,\varepsilon}\right)\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}}\right)_{\varepsilon}\subset\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}-R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}|,\varepsilon}\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}}\right)_{\varepsilon}\subset\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)^{c}\subset\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}\right)^{c}\subset\left(\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{c}\\
&\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}+\left(t-R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}|,\varepsilon}\right)\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}\right)_{\varepsilon}\subset\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}-R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}|,\varepsilon}\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}\right)_{\varepsilon}\subset\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\right)^{c}\subset\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}}\right)^{c}\subset\left(\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{c}.\end{split}

(4.11)

Furthermore, for any
t\geq 0
, and
t_{1},t_{2},t_{1}^{\prime},t_{2}^{\prime}
satisfying (
4.10
) for this
t
,
from Lemma
4.2
, we have

\displaystyle S_{2}^{t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}}\in\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\left(\left(\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}\right)\cup\left(\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}+t_{2}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\right)^{c}}\right)^{\prime}\subset\pi_{0}({\mathcal{A}}_{(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}})^{c}})^{\prime}=\mathfrak{A}(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}}).

(4.12)

Similarly, we have
S_{1}^{t_{1},t_{1}^{\prime}}\in\mathfrak{A}(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}).

From this and the approximate Haag duality, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}},\varepsilon}^{*}\right)\left(S_{2}^{t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\in\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+\left(t-R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}|,\varepsilon}\right)\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}}\right)_{\varepsilon}}\right)^{\prime\prime}\subset\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\left(\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{c}}\right)^{\prime\prime},\\
\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}},\varepsilon}^{*}\right)\left(S_{1}^{t_{1},t_{1}^{\prime}}\right)\in\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}+\left(t-R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}|,\varepsilon}\right)\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}\right)_{\varepsilon}}\right)^{\prime\prime}\subset\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda_{\left(\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{c}}}\right)^{\prime\prime}.\end{split}

(4.13)

Therefore, by Lemma
2.4

(
U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}},\varepsilon}\in{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
),
Lemma
2.14
(i) (
T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}
is
\sigma
w-continuous on
\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+\left(t-R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}|,\varepsilon}\right)\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}}\right)_{\varepsilon}}\right)^{\prime\prime}

) (d) and (
4.13
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(S_{2}^{t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\\
&=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}},\varepsilon}\right)\right)\circ T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}},\varepsilon}^{*}\right)\left(S_{2}^{t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}}\right)\right)\\
&=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}},\varepsilon}\right)U^{*}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}},\varepsilon}\right)\left(S_{2}^{t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}}\right).\end{split}

(4.14)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&T_{\rho_{2}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{2},\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left(S_{1}^{t_{1},t_{1}^{\prime}}\right)\\
&=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(T_{\rho_{2}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{2},\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left(U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}},\varepsilon}\right)U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}},\varepsilon}^{*}\right)\left(S_{1}^{t_{1},t_{1}^{\prime}}\right).\end{split}

(4.15)

From Lemma
2.18
applied to
\Lambda_{1}
,
\Lambda_{2}

t
replaced by
\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}
,

\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}}
,
\frac{t}{2}
,
we have

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}},\varepsilon}\right)U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}},\varepsilon}^{*}-\mathbb{I}\right\|\leq 2f_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}|,\varepsilon,\delta}\left(\frac{t}{2}\right),

(4.16)

for any
t\geq 0
and

t_{1},t_{1}^{\prime},t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}\leq 0

satisfying (
4.10
)
for this
t
.

Now, for any
\epsilon^{\prime}>0
, choose
t>0
such that

2f_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}|,\varepsilon,\delta}(\frac{t}{2})<\epsilon^{\prime}
.
Then for any
t_{1},t_{2},t_{1}^{\prime},t_{2}^{\prime}
satisfying (
4.10
) for this
t

we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left\|T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(S_{2}^{t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}}\right)-S_{2}^{t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}}\right\|\\
&\leq 2\left\|T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}},\varepsilon}\right)U_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}+t\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}},\varepsilon}^{*}-\mathbb{I}\right\|\leq 4f_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}|,\varepsilon,\delta}\left(\frac{t}{2}\right)<4\epsilon^{\prime}.\end{split}

(4.17)

Hence we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lim_{t_{1},t_{2},t_{1}^{\prime},t_{2}^{\prime}\to\infty}\left\|T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(S_{2}^{t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}}\right)-S_{2}^{t_{2},t_{2}^{\prime}}\right\|=0.\end{split}

(4.18)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lim_{t_{1},t_{2},t_{1}^{\prime},t_{2}^{\prime}\to\infty}\left\|T_{\rho_{2}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{2},\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left(S_{1}^{t_{1},t_{1}^{\prime}}\right)-S_{1}^{t_{1},t_{1}^{\prime}}\right\|=0.\end{split}

(4.19)

Combinig this with Lemma
2.8
and (
4.12
), we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lim_{t_{1},t_{2},t_{1}^{\prime},t_{2}^{\prime}\to\infty}\left\|S_{1}^{t_{1}t_{1}^{\prime}}\otimes S_{2}^{t_{2}t_{2}^{\prime}}-S_{2}^{t_{2}t_{2}^{\prime}}\otimes S_{1}^{t_{1}t_{1}^{\prime}}\right\|\\
&=\lim_{t_{1},t_{2},t_{1}^{\prime},t_{2}^{\prime}\to\infty}\left\|S_{1}^{t_{1}t_{1}^{\prime}}T_{\rho_{1}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{1},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}(S_{2}^{t_{2}t_{2}^{\prime}})-S_{2}^{t_{2}t_{2}^{\prime}}T_{\rho_{2}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{2},\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}(S_{1}^{t_{1}t_{1}^{\prime}})\right\|=0.\end{split}

(4.20)

\square

Definition 4.7
.

Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
.
We write
\Lambda_{2}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}
if

\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}

can be written
\Lambda_{i}=\Lambda_{\bm{p}_{i},\theta_{i},\varphi_{i}}
,
i=1,2

with
\theta_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi_{i}\in(0,\pi)
satisfying

\displaystyle\theta+\varphi<\theta_{1}-\varphi_{1}<\theta_{1}+\varphi_{1}<\theta_{2}-\varphi_{2}<\theta_{2}+\varphi_{2}<\theta-\varphi+2\pi.

(4.21)

Lemma 4.8
.

Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
.
For any
\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2},\Lambda_{1}^{\prime},\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}

with
\Lambda_{1}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{2}
,

\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}
,

\Lambda_{2}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}
, and
\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}
,
there are
\{\Lambda_{i}^{(j)}\}_{j=0}^{4},\{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j)}\}_{j=0}^{4}\subset{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

i=1,2

such that

\displaystyle\{\Lambda_{1}^{(j)},\Lambda_{1}^{(j+1)}\}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\{\Lambda_{2}^{(j)},\Lambda_{2}^{(j+1)}\},\quad\{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}^{(j)},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}^{(j+1)}\}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}^{(j)},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}^{(j+1)}\},\quad j=0,1,2,3

(4.22)

\displaystyle\{\Lambda_{1}^{(4)},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}^{(4)}\}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\{\Lambda_{2}^{(4)},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}^{(4)}\}

(4.23)

\displaystyle\Lambda_{i}^{(0)}=\Lambda_{i},\quad{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(0)}=\Lambda_{i}^{\prime},\quad i=1,2.

(4.24)

Set
\Lambda_{i}^{(0)}=\Lambda_{i}
,
i=1,2
.

, there are

,

.

\Lambda_{2}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}
,

,
i=1,2
with

\theta_{i},\varphi_{i}

satisfying (
4.21
).
Set
\Lambda_{i}^{(1)}:=\Lambda_{\bm{p}_{i},\theta_{i},\frac{\varphi_{i}}{4}}
,
i=1,2
.
Then because of

\Lambda_{i}^{(1)}\subset\Lambda_{i}\subset\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}
,
i=1,2
,
we have
\{\Lambda_{1}^{(0)},\Lambda_{1}^{(1)}\}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\{\Lambda_{2}^{(0)},\Lambda_{2}^{(1)}\}
.

Next, note

{\mathbb{A}}_{[\theta_{2}-\varphi_{2},\theta-\varphi+2\pi-\frac{\varphi_{2}}{4}]}
,

{\mathbb{A}}_{[\theta+\varphi+\frac{\varphi_{1}}{4},\theta_{1}+\varphi_{1}]}
,

\Lambda_{2}^{(1)}
,
\Lambda_{\bm{p}_{2},\theta-\varphi+2\pi-\frac{\varphi_{2}}{2},\frac{\varphi_{2}}{8}}
,

,

satisfy the conditions of

{\mathbb{A}}_{1}
,
{\mathbb{A}}_{2}
,
\Lambda_{1}
,
\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}
,
\Lambda_{2}
,
\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}

in Lemma
2.9
.
Applying the latter lemma, we obtain

L_{1},L_{1}^{\prime},L_{2},L_{2}^{\prime}\geq 0

such that
\{\Lambda_{1}^{(2)},\Lambda_{1}^{(3)}\}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\{\Lambda_{2}^{(2)},\Lambda_{2}^{(3)}\}

for

\displaystyle\Lambda_{i}^{(2)}:=\Lambda^{(1)}_{i}+L_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda^{(1)}_{i}},\quad i=1,2

(4.25)

\displaystyle\Lambda_{2}^{(3)}:=\Lambda_{\bm{p}_{2},\theta-\varphi+2\pi-\frac{\varphi_{2}}{2},\frac{\varphi_{2}}{8}}+L_{1}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\theta-\varphi+2\pi-\frac{\varphi_{2}}{2}},

(4.26)

\displaystyle\Lambda_{1}^{(3)}:=\Lambda_{\bm{p}_{1},\theta+\varphi+\frac{\varphi_{1}}{2},\frac{\varphi_{1}}{8}}+L_{2}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\theta+\varphi+\frac{\varphi_{1}}{2}}.

(4.27)

Note also that from
\Lambda_{i}^{(2)}\subset\Lambda_{i}^{(1)}\subset\tilde{\Lambda}_{i}
,
we have

,

,

such that

,

,

,
and

(4.29)

Choose
\delta>0
small enough so that

\displaystyle\arg\Lambda_{2}^{(3)},\arg{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}^{(3)}\subset{\mathbb{A}}_{[\theta+\frac{11}{8}\pi-\frac{3}{8}\varphi,\theta+2\pi-\varphi-\delta]},\quad\arg\Lambda_{1}^{(3)},\arg{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}^{(3)}\subset{\mathbb{A}}_{[\theta+\varphi+\delta,\theta+\frac{5}{8}\pi+\frac{3}{8}\varphi]},

\Lambda_{2}^{(3)}
,
{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}^{(3)}

,

\Lambda_{1}
,

\Lambda_{2}

\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}

in Lemma

Lemma 4.9
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
, and

\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2},\Lambda_{1}^{\prime},\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}

satisfying

\Lambda_{1}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{2}
,

\Lambda^{\prime}_{1}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda^{\prime}_{2}

and
\Lambda_{2}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}
,

\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}
.
Let
\rho_{i},\rho_{i}^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
,

V_{\rho_{i}\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{i}\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}
,

V_{\rho_{i}^{\prime}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho_{i}^{\prime}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}
,
and

R_{i}^{t_{i},t_{i}^{\prime}}\in\left(T_{\rho_{i}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{i},\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}},T_{\rho_{i}^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}+t_{i}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{i}^{\prime},\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}+t_{i}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{\prime}}}}\right),\quad\left\|R_{i}^{t_{i},t_{i}^{\prime}}\right\|\leq 1

given for each
t_{i},t_{i}^{\prime}\geq 0

and
i=1,2
.
Then we have

\displaystyle\lim_{t_{1},t_{2},t_{1}^{\prime}t_{2}^{\prime}\to\infty}\left\|R_{1}^{t_{1}t_{1}^{\prime}}\otimes R_{2}^{t_{2}t_{2}^{\prime}}-R_{2}^{t_{2}t_{2}^{\prime}}\otimes R_{1}^{t_{1}t_{1}^{\prime}}\right\|=0.

(4.32)

By Lemma
4.8
, there are

\{\Lambda_{i}^{(j)}\}_{j=0}^{4},\{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j)}\}_{j=0}^{4}\subset{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,

i=1,2
satisfying (
4.22
), (
4.23
), (
4.24
).
For
t_{ij},t^{\prime}_{ij}\geq 0
,
i=1,2
,
j=0,\ldots 4
, we denote the set
\bm{t}:=\left((t_{ij}),(t^{\prime}_{ij})\right)

and
\bm{t}\to\infty
means
t_{i,j},t_{ij}^{\prime}\to\infty
for all
i,j
.
Set

\displaystyle W_{i}^{(j),\bm{t}}:=\bar{V}_{\rho_{i}\Lambda_{i}^{(j+1)}+t_{i,j+1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{(j+1)}}}{\left(\bar{V}_{\rho_{i}\Lambda_{i}^{(j)}+t_{ij}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{(j)}}}\right)^{*}},\quad\text{
and}\quad{W^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j),\bm{t}}:=\bar{V}_{\rho_{i}^{\prime}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j+1)}+t^{\prime}_{i,j+1}\bm{e}_{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j+1)}}}{\left(\bar{V}_{\rho^{\prime}_{i}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j)}+t^{\prime}_{ij}\bm{e}_{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j)}}}\right)^{*}}.

(4.33)

We have

\displaystyle\begin{split}W_{i}^{(j),\bm{t}}\in\left(T_{\rho_{i}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i}^{(j)}+t_{ij}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{(j)}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{i},\Lambda_{i}^{(j)}+t_{ij}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{(j)}}}},T_{\rho_{i}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i}^{(j+1)}+t_{i,j+1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{(j+1)}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{i},\Lambda_{i}^{(j+1)}+t_{i,j+1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{(j+1)}}}}\right),\\
{W^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j),\bm{t}}\in\left(T_{\rho_{i}^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j)}+t_{ij}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j)}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{i}^{\prime},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j)}+t_{ij}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j)}}}},T_{\rho_{i}^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j+1)}+t_{i,j+1}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j+1)}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{i}^{\prime},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j+1)}+t_{i,j+1}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(j+1)}}}}\right)\end{split}

(4.34)

for
i=1,2
and
j=0,1,2,3
from Lemma
4.1
.
By Lemma
4.6
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|W_{1}^{(j),\bm{t}}\otimes W_{2}^{(j),\bm{t}}-W_{2}^{(j)\bm{t}}\otimes W_{1}^{(j)\bm{t}}\right\|=0,\quad\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|{W^{\prime}}_{1}^{(j),\bm{t}}\otimes{W^{\prime}}_{2}^{(j),\bm{t}}-{W^{\prime}}_{2}^{(j)\bm{t}}\otimes{W^{\prime}}_{1}^{(j)\bm{t}}\right\|=0\quad j=0,1,2,3.

(4.35)

Note that

\displaystyle\begin{split}S_{i}^{\bm{t}}:={W^{\prime}}_{i}^{(3)\bm{t}}{W^{\prime}}_{i}^{(2)\bm{t}}{W^{\prime}}_{i}^{(1)\bm{t}}{W^{\prime}}_{i}^{(0)\bm{t}}R_{i}^{(t_{i_{0}}t_{i_{0}}^{\prime})}\left(W_{i}^{(3)\bm{t}}W_{i}^{(2)\bm{t}}W_{i}^{(1)\bm{t}}W_{i}^{(0)\bm{t}}\right)^{*}\\
\in\left(T_{\rho_{i}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{i}^{(4)}+t_{i4}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{(4)}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{i},\Lambda_{i}^{(4)}+t_{i4}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}^{(4)}}}},T_{\rho_{i}^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(4)}+t_{i4}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(4)}},\bar{V}_{\rho_{i}^{\prime},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(4)}+t_{i4}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{i}^{(4)}}}}\right).\end{split}

(4.36)

Because
\{\Lambda_{1}^{(4)},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}^{(4)}\}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\{\Lambda_{2}^{(4)},{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}^{(4)}\}
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|S_{1}^{\bm{t}}\otimes S_{2}^{\bm{t}}-S_{2}^{\bm{t}}\otimes S_{1}^{\bm{t}}\right\|=0

(4.37)

by Lemma
4.6
.

By Lemma
4.5
and (
4.35
), (
4.37
) we complete the proof.

\square

Lemma 4.10
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.

,

,

and
\bar{V}_{\rho\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}

,

,

,

\Lambda_{1}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{2}
,

and

{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}
,
{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}
,
then we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}\to\infty}\left\|\left(W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{\bm{t}}\otimes W_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}\right)^{*}\left(W_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}\otimes W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{\bm{t}}\right)-\left(W_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}^{\bm{t}^{\prime}}\otimes W_{\rho{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}^{\bm{t}^{\prime}}\right)^{*}\left(W_{\rho{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}^{\bm{t}^{\prime}}\otimes W_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}^{\bm{t}^{\prime}}\right)\right\|=0.

(4.38)

Here,
\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}\to\infty
means

t_{i},t_{i}^{\prime}\to\infty
for each
i
.

Definition 4.11
.

From Lemma
4.10
, for any
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,

\rho,\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and
\Lambda_{0}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}

we may define

\displaystyle\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}(\rho,\sigma):=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left(W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{\bm{t}}\otimes W_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}\right)^{*}\left(W_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}\otimes W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{\bm{t}}\right)\in{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)},

(4.39)

independent of the choice of
\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,
satisfying
\Lambda_{1}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{2}
,
\Lambda_{2}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}
,
and
V_{\rho\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}
,
V_{\sigma\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}
.

Set
W_{\rho\Lambda_{1}\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}}^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}:=\bar{V}_{\rho\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}+t_{1}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda^{\prime}_{1}}}\bar{V}_{\rho\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}^{*}
,

W_{\sigma\Lambda_{2}\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}}^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}:=\bar{V}_{\sigma\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}+t_{2}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda^{\prime}_{2}}}\bar{V}_{\sigma\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}^{*}
.
From Lemma
4.1
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&W^{\bm{t}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}},T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\right),\quad W^{\bm{t}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\in\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}},T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\right),\\
&W^{\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}},T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}+t_{1}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}+t_{1}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}}}}\right),\quad W^{\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}\in\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}},T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}+t_{2}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}+t_{2}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}}}}\right),\\
&W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho\Lambda_{1}\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}}\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}},T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}+t_{1}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}+t_{1}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}}}}\right),\\
&W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{2}\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}}\in\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}},T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}+t_{2}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}+t_{2}^{\prime}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}}}}\right).\end{split}

(4.40)

By Lemma
4.5
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left(W^{\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}\otimes W^{\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}\right)^{*}\left(W^{\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}\otimes W^{\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}\right)\\
&=\left(W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{2}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}W^{\bm{t}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda}_{2}}\otimes W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{1}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}W^{\bm{t}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda}_{1}}\right)^{*}\left(W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{1}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}W^{\bm{t}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda}_{1}}\otimes W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{2}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}W^{\bm{t}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda}_{2}}\right)\\
&=\left(W^{\bm{t}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda}_{2}}\otimes W^{\bm{t}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda}_{1}}\right)^{*}\left(W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{2}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}\otimes W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{1}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}\right)^{*}\left(W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{1}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}\otimes W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{2}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}\right)\left(W^{\bm{t}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda}_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{0}{\Lambda}_{2}}\right).\end{split}

(4.41)

Note that
setting
\rho_{1}:=\rho
,
\rho_{1}^{\prime}:=\rho
,
\rho_{2}:=\sigma
,
\rho_{2}^{\prime}:=\sigma

and
R_{1}:=W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho\Lambda_{1}\Lambda_{1}^{\prime}}

R_{2}:=W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{2}\Lambda_{2}^{\prime}}

in Lemma
4.9
, we obtain

\displaystyle\lim_{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}\to\infty}\left\|\left(W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{2}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}\otimes W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{1}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}\right)^{*}\left(W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\rho{\Lambda}_{1}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{1}}\otimes W^{\bm{t},\bm{t}^{\prime}}_{\sigma{\Lambda}_{2}{\Lambda^{\prime}}_{2}}\right)-\mathbb{I}\right\|=0.

(4.42)

Substituting this to (
4.41
), we prove the claim.

\square

Lemma 4.12
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
.
For any
\rho,\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
and
\Lambda_{0}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}

we have

\displaystyle\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}(\rho,\sigma)\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}},T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\right).

(4.43)

Let

\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,
be cones satisfying
\Lambda_{1}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{2}
,
\Lambda_{2}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}
.
We use the notation in Lemma
4.10
.
Note that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}},T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\right),\\
&\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\in\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}},T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\right).\end{split}

(4.44)

Note also that by Lemma
2.14
(d), we have
for any
A\in{\mathcal{A}}_{loc}
,
we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|\begin{gathered}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\\
-T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\end{gathered}\right\|=0,

because the support of
A
gets out of
\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}

eventually.
For any
A\in{\mathcal{A}}_{loc}

we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}(\rho,\sigma)T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right),\end{split}

(4.47)

and

\displaystyle\begin{split}&T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}(\rho,\sigma)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right).\end{split}

(4.48)

From this and (
4
), we obtain

\displaystyle\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}(\rho,\sigma)T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)=T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}(\rho,\sigma)

(4.49)

for any
A\in{\mathcal{A}}_{\rm loc}
.
Using the
\sigma
w-continuity of
T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}
,

T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}
(Lemma
3.5
) and Lemma
2.4

we can replace
\pi_{0}(A)

by general
x\in{\mathcal{B}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
, proving the claim.

\square

Lemma 4.13
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
.
For any
\rho,\sigma,\rho^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
,
\Lambda_{0}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,
and
R\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}},T_{\rho^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho^{\prime},\Lambda_{0}}}\right)
,

S\in\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}},T_{\sigma^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma^{\prime},\Lambda_{0}}}\right)
,
we have

\displaystyle\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}(\rho^{\prime},\sigma^{\prime})(R\otimes S)=(S\otimes R)\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}(\rho,\sigma).

(4.50)

Let

\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,
be cones satisfying
\Lambda_{2}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}
,
\Lambda_{2}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}
.
We use the notation in Lemma
4.10
.
Note that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}R{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\in\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}},T_{\rho^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho^{\prime},\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\right),\\
&{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}S{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}\in\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}},T_{\sigma^{\prime}}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma^{\prime},\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\right).\end{split}

(4.51)

Because
\Lambda_{1}\perp_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{2}
by Lemma
4.9
, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}R{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}S{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}-{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}S{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}R{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\right\|=0.

(4.52)

From this and Lemma
4.5
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)(R\otimes S)\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{*}-\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)(S\otimes R)\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\right\|\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}R{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}S{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}-{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}S{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho^{\prime}\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}R{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\right\|=0.\end{split}

(4.53)

This proves the Lemma.

\square

Lemma 4.14
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
, and

\rho,\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
,

\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,
and
V_{\rho\Lambda_{i}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho\Lambda_{i}}
,
V_{\sigma\Lambda_{i}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{i}}
,

i=0,1
.
Set
W_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}:=V_{\rho\Lambda_{1}}V_{\rho\Lambda_{0}}^{*}
,

W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}:=V_{\sigma\Lambda_{1}}V_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}}^{*}
.
Fix some
\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{i}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\eta,\Lambda_{i}}\mid\eta\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}\}
,

i=0,1
.
Then we have

\displaystyle W_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma,\Lambda_{1}},

(4.54)

\displaystyle 1\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}.

(4.55)

Remark 4.15
.

By this Lemma and Definition
4.11
and Lemma
4.10
,
we may set

W^{\bm{t}}_{\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1}}:=W_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}\otimes W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}},

in the definition of
\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma,\cdot\right)

and
\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\cdot,\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma\right)
.

For any
A\in{\mathcal{A}}_{\Lambda_{1}^{c}}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(W_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma(A)\\
&=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(W_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)\right)T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\\
&=\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(W_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\mathop{\mathrm{Ad}}\nolimits\left(W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)\\
&=T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{1}}}\left(\pi_{0}(A)\right)=\pi_{0}(A)\end{split}

(4.56)

In the third equality, we used Lemma
2.15
(iii).
In the fourth equality, we used Lemma
2.14
(d).
Hence we obtain (
4.54
).
The latter one (
4.55
)
is proven in Lemma
3.6
.

\square

Lemma 4.16
.

Consider Setting
2.1
and assume the approximate Haag duality.
Let
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
,
\Lambda_{0}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
and
fix
\{\bar{V}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\eta\Lambda_{0}}\mid\eta\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}\}
.
For any
\rho,\sigma,\tau\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}
,
we have

\displaystyle\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\sigma,\tau\right)=\left(\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho,\tau\right)\otimes 1_{T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}}\right)\left(1_{T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}}\otimes\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\sigma,\tau\right)\right),

(4.57)

\displaystyle\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho,\sigma\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\tau\right)=\left(1_{T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}}\otimes\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho,\tau\right)\right)\left(\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho,\sigma\right)\otimes 1_{T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{0}}}}\right).

(4.58)

First we prove (
4.57
).
We may take

\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
,
satisfying
\Lambda_{2}\leftarrow_{(\theta,\varphi)}\Lambda_{1}

so that there are some
\tilde{\Lambda}_{1},\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&(\Lambda_{1})_{\varepsilon},\Lambda_{0}\subset\tilde{\Lambda}_{1},\quad\Lambda_{2}\subset\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\\
&\arg\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)_{2\varepsilon+\delta+\epsilon+\epsilon^{\prime}}\cap\arg\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\right)_{2\varepsilon+\delta+\epsilon+\epsilon^{\prime}}=\emptyset,\\
&\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}-R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}|,\varepsilon}\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}\right)_{\varepsilon}\subset\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}^{c},\quad\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}-R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}|,\varepsilon}\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}}\right)_{\varepsilon}\subset\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}^{c},\\
&\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)_{2(\varepsilon+\epsilon+\epsilon^{\prime}+\delta)},\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{2}\right)_{2(\varepsilon+\epsilon+\epsilon^{\prime}+\delta)}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)},\end{split}

(4.59)

for some
\delta,\varepsilon>0
and
\epsilon,\epsilon^{\prime}>0
small enough.

Fix some

V_{\rho\Lambda_{0}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}}
,

V_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}}
,

V_{\tau\Lambda_{0}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}}
,

V_{\rho\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\rho\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}
,

V_{\sigma\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}
,

V_{\tau\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\tau\Lambda_{i}+t_{i}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}
,
for
t_{i}\geq 0

i=1,2
and set

{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{i}\Lambda_{j}}:=V_{\rho\Lambda_{j}+{t_{j}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{j}}}V_{\rho\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}^{*}
,

{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{i}\Lambda_{j}}:=V_{\sigma\Lambda_{j}+{t_{j}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{j}}}V_{\sigma\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}^{*}
,

{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{i}\Lambda_{j}}:=V_{\tau\Lambda_{j}+{t_{j}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{j}}}V_{\tau\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}^{*}
,
if
i,j=1,2
.
Set also

{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{i}}:=V_{\rho\Lambda_{i}+{t_{i}}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{i}}}V_{\rho\Lambda_{0}}^{*}
,
for
i=1,2
.
By Remark
4.15
, we may set

\displaystyle\begin{split}&{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{1}}:={W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}={W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)\\
&{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\tau,\Lambda_{0},\Lambda_{2}}:={W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}={W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right),\end{split}

(4.60)

in the definition of
\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma,\tau\right)

and
\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho,\sigma\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\tau\right)
.

Fix any
\epsilon^{\prime\prime}>0
.
Choose
s\geq R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}|+2(\varepsilon+\delta+\epsilon),\frac{\epsilon^{\prime}}{2}},R_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}|,\varepsilon}

such that

\displaystyle 2f_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}|+2(\varepsilon+\delta+\epsilon),\frac{\epsilon^{\prime}}{2},\frac{\epsilon^{\prime}}{2}}(s)<\epsilon^{\prime\prime},\quad 2f_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}|,\varepsilon,\delta}(s)<\epsilon^{\prime\prime}.

(4.61)

Because

\displaystyle W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}\in\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\left(\left(\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}\right)\cup\Lambda_{0}\right)^{c}}\right)^{\prime}\subset\mathfrak{A}\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right),

(4.62)

from Lemma
2.5
,
there is a unitary
\tilde{W}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}s}\in\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)_{\varepsilon+\delta}-s\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}}\right)^{\prime\prime}

such that

\displaystyle\left\|W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}-\tilde{W}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}s}\right\|\leq 2f_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}|,\varepsilon,\delta}(s)<\epsilon^{\prime\prime}.

(4.63)

From Lemma
2.16
,
we have

\displaystyle T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(\tilde{W}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}s}\right)\in\mathfrak{A}\left(\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)_{\varepsilon+\delta+\epsilon}-s\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}\right).

(4.64)

Then by Lemma
2.5
, there is a unitary

\displaystyle X_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}s}\in\pi_{0}\left({\mathcal{A}}_{\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)_{\varepsilon+\delta+\epsilon+\epsilon^{\prime}}-2s\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}}\right)^{\prime\prime}

(4.65)

such that

\displaystyle\left\|X_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}s}-T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(\tilde{W}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}s}\right)\right\|\leq 2f_{|\arg\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}|+2(\varepsilon+\delta+\epsilon),\frac{\epsilon^{\prime}}{2},\frac{\epsilon^{\prime}}{2}}(s)<\epsilon^{\prime\prime}.

(4.66)

For
t_{2}
large enough, we have

\displaystyle\left(\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}\right)_{\varepsilon+\delta+\epsilon+\epsilon^{\prime}}-2s\bm{e}_{\tilde{\Lambda}_{1}}\subset\left(\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{c},

(4.67)

and

\displaystyle T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left(X_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}s}\right)=X_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}s}.

(4.68)

Hence we obtain

\displaystyle\lim\sup_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}\right)-T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}\right)\right\|\leq 2\epsilon^{\prime\prime}.

(4.69)

As this holds for any
\epsilon^{\prime\prime}>0
,
we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(W_{\sigma\Lambda_{1}\Lambda_{0}}^{\bm{t}}\right)-T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(W_{\sigma\Lambda_{1}\Lambda_{0}}^{\bm{t}}\right)\right\|=0.

(4.70)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left(W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}\right)-W_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{\bm{t}}\right\|=0.

(4.71)

Hence we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left\|\begin{gathered}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)\\
-T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)\end{gathered}\right\|=0.\end{split}

(4.72)

By the definition of
\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}

and the fact that
{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{i}\Lambda_{j}}
etc are intertwiners, we obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left(\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho,\tau\right)\otimes 1_{T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}}\right)\left(1_{T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}}\otimes\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\sigma,\tau\right)\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\right){W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\\
&\quad T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\right){W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)\\
&\quad T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\right)\\
&\quad T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*})\right)T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right){W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\\
&\quad T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\right)\\
&\quad T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\right)T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right){W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\\
&\quad T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\right)\right)\\
&T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right){W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\quad T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\right){W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}^{*}\right){W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)\\
&\quad T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\circ_{\Lambda_{0}}\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\right){W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}^{*}{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\circ_{\Lambda_{0}}\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\circ_{\Lambda_{0}}\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\circ_{\Lambda_{0}}\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho\circ_{\Lambda_{0}}\sigma,\tau\right).\end{split}

(4.73)

In the third equality, we used (
4.72
).
In the sixth equality, we used (
4.60
).
This proves the first equality (
4.57
).

The second one (
4.58
) can be proven analogously.
As in the first case, we can choose
\Lambda_{1},\Lambda_{2}

so that

\displaystyle\left\|\begin{gathered}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\right)\\
-T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\end{gathered}\right\|\to 0,\quad\bm{t}\to\infty.

We have

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left(1_{T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}}\otimes\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho,\tau\right)\right)\left(\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho,\sigma\right)\otimes 1_{T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{0}}}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\right)T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{*}\right)\\
&\quad T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\right)\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{*}{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\right)T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{*}\right)\\
&\quad\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{*}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left(T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\right){W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\right)T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{*}\right)\\
&\quad\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{*}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{1}+t_{1}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{1}}}}\left(T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{2}+t_{2}\bm{e}_{\Lambda_{2}}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\right){W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}T_{\sigma}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\sigma,\Lambda_{0}}}T_{\tau}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\tau,\Lambda_{0}}}\left(\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\right)\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\circ_{\Lambda_{0}}\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)^{*}\\
&\quad{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}T_{\rho}^{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\bar{V}_{\rho,\Lambda_{0}}}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\circ_{\Lambda_{0}}\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\lim_{\bm{t}\to\infty}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\circ_{\Lambda}\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\right)^{*}\left({W^{\bm{t}}}_{\rho\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{1}}\otimes{W^{\bm{t}}}_{\sigma\circ_{\Lambda}\tau\Lambda_{0}\Lambda_{2}}\right)\\
&=\epsilon_{+}^{(\Lambda_{0})}\left(\rho,\sigma\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\tau\right).\end{split}

(4.76)

\square

5
Braided
C^{*}
-tensor category

Let us consider the following setting.
For the definition of interactions and the dynamics given by them, see
[
BR2
]
.

Setting 5.1
.

Let
\Phi
be a uniformly bounded finite range interaction on
{\mathcal{A}}_{{\mathbb{Z}}^{2}}
and
\tau_{\Phi}
the
C^{*}
-dynamics given by
\Phi
.
Let
\omega
be a pure
\tau_{\Phi}
-ground state satisfying the gap condition:
there is a
\gamma>0
such that

\displaystyle-i\omega\left(B^{*}\delta_{\Phi}(B)\right)\geq\gamma\omega(B^{*}B),

(5.1)

for any
B\in{\mathcal{A}}_{{\mathbb{Z}}^{2}}
in the domain of the generator
\delta_{\Phi}
of
\tau_{\Phi}
with
\omega(B)=0
.
Let
({\mathcal{H}},\pi_{0},\Omega)
be a GNS representation of
\omega
.
We use the notations in Setting
2.1
for this
({\mathcal{H}},\pi_{0})
.
We assume that
\pi_{0}
has a nontrivial sector theory.
We also assume the approximate Haag duality for
\pi_{0}
.
Fix some
\theta\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
\varphi\in(0,\pi)
and
\Lambda_{0}\in{\mathcal{C}}_{(\theta,\varphi)}
.
We set

\displaystyle{\mathcal{O}}_{\Lambda_{0}}:=\left\{\rho\in{\mathcal{O}}_{0}\mid\rho|_{\Lambda_{0}^{c}}=\pi_{0}|_{\Lambda_{0}^{c}}\right\}.

(5.2)

We define the multiplication of
\rho,\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{\Lambda_{0}}

by
\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma
in Definition
3.1
,
with
\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}:=\mathbb{I}_{{\mathcal{H}}}\in{\mathcal{V}}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}
,
for each
\eta\in{\mathcal{O}}_{\Lambda_{0}}
.
Note from Lemma
3.3
that in fact

\rho\;\;\circ_{{(\theta,\varphi),\Lambda_{0},\{\bar{V}_{\eta,\Lambda_{0}}\}_{\eta}}}\;\;\sigma\in{\mathcal{O}}_{\Lambda_{0}}
.

In this section we show the following theorem.
For definition of a braided
C^{*}
-tensor category, see

[
NT
]
.

Theorem 5.2
.

Consider Setting
5.1
.
Then
{\mathcal{O}}_{\Lambda_{0}}
and its morphisms form
a braided
C^{*}
-tensor category.

We already have tensor (i.e., composition) and braiding.
What is missing is direct sums and subrepresentations.
In order to do that we need to prove that
